### [1] [Boundary-contact repolarization provides a mechanism for directional guidance of neural crest cell collectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12721)
*Aditya Shankar Paspunurwar,Hector Gomez*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过计算模型揭示：神经嵴细胞集体迁移不仅依赖空间限制，还需细胞-边界接触诱导的极性重定向（接触介导的再极化）才能维持持续定向迁移，建立了一个可实验验证的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经嵴细胞（NCCs）是研究胚胎发育中集体迁移的经典系统，其迁移行为与癌症侵袭类似。已有研究确立了共吸引、运动接触抑制、极性持续性和组织限制在引导NCC迁移中的作用，但尚不清楚受限环境中的NCC迁移是仅由空间限制驱动，还是也涉及因与限制边界接触而诱导的再极化响应。

Method: 构建了一个计算模型，将自生成化学信号传输与接触介导的再极化及机械相互作用相耦合，用以研究受限条件下NCC的集体迁移行为。

Result: 细胞-边界接触能够重新定向细胞极性并在受限条件下维持定向迁移；而无受限时则不出现持续的集体方向性。此外，在保留机械相互作用的同时抑制来自非允许性限制边界的接触介导再极化，会阻止持续定向迁移，表明在模型中仅靠空间限制不足。

Conclusion: 这些发现建立了一个可实验验证的机制：自生成化学信号与限制组织的接触共同调控NCC的集体引导。该见解为研究发育性迁移缺陷及调控集体组织侵袭的潜在策略提供了基础。

Abstract: Neural crest cells (NCCs) provide a well-established system for investigating collective migration during embryonic development and exhibit migratory behaviors with parallels to cancer invasion. Previous studies establish roles for co-attraction, contact inhibition of locomotion, polarity persistence, and tissue confinement in guiding NCC migration. However, it remains unclear whether NCC migration in confined environments occurs through spatial restriction alone or also through a repolarization response induced by contact with boundaries of confinement. By developing a computational model that couples self-generated chemical transport with contact-mediated repolarization and mechanical interactions, we show that cell-boundary contact can reorient cell polarity and sustain directional migration under confinement, whereas no persistent collective direction emerges without confinement. Moreover, suppressing contact-mediated repolarization from non-permissive confinement boundaries while preserving the mechanical interactions prevents sustained directional migration, suggesting that spatial restriction alone is insufficient in the model. Together, these findings establish an experimentally testable mechanism through which self-generated chemical signals and contact with confining tissues jointly regulate collective guidance of NCCs. These insights provide a basis for investigating developmental migration defects and potential strategies to regulate collective tissue invasion.

</details>


### [2] [Efficient Transport Networks through Edge Pruning by Relative Resistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13582)
*Mathieu Le Verge-Serandour,Viktor Vasilev,Lara Szeimies,Karen Alim*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 受生物运输网络启发，提出一种仅依赖相对电阻进行局部剪枝的自适应机制：迭代移除电阻高于绕行路径的边缘，去除该边缘后其余网络仍可路由流量。该机制无需全局目标或特定边界条件，由拓扑决定冗余度，最终生成稀疏、多环、低耗散的运输网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运输网络是生命系统和工程系统的核心。核心问题是：运输网络能否仅靠局部规则高效自适应，而不依赖全局目标函数或特定边界条件？生物运输网络为此提供了灵感。

Method: 采用基于相对电阻的迭代剪枝：对每条边，比较其自身电阻与"绕行"路由流量（即经由网络其余部分）所需的电阻；若自身电阻更高，则移除该边。该冗余度量是局部的、由拓扑单独决定，不涉及全局目标或边界条件设定。

Result: 通过上述局部、无边界条件的冗余度量进行自适应后，网络演化为稀疏、环丰富（loop-rich）且低耗散的运输网络。

Conclusion: 结果表明，无需全局优化目标或特定边界条件，仅凭由拓扑决定的局部冗余判据即可实现高效网络自适应，揭示了生物运输网络高效性的可能机制，并为工程网络设计提供思路。

Abstract: Transport networks are key to living and engineered systems. How can transport networks adapt efficiently using only local rules, without a global objective or specific boundary conditions? Inspired by observations in biological transport networks, we adapt networks by subsequently pruning edges by relative resistance, thus removing edges whose resistance is higher than the resistance for routing flow through the rest of the network. We find that adaptation by such a local, boundary-condition-free measure of redundancy, set by topology alone, yields sparse, loop-rich, low-dissipation networks.

</details>

### [3] [Neuromorphic Satellite Intelligence: A Formal Framework for Event-Driven Architectures, Spike-Based Algorithms, and Quantitative Energy-Accuracy Trade-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12471)
*Suresh Kumar TP,Vikas Agnihotri*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 该文构建脉冲神经网络（SNN）星载计算的形式化能效模型，提出三种原创算法与能效-精度-时延（EAL）权衡曲面，结合抗辐射架构分类与在轨STDP学习规则，经三类卫星任务案例验证，可在精度损失≤2.1%前提下较GPU基线实现8–47倍能耗降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星载计算存在根本性矛盾：任务智能化需求持续增长，而功率、质量与辐射预算固定不变。传统GPU/FPGA方案能耗与算力扩展受限，需探索事件驱动、稀疏激活的神经形态计算以突破能效瓶颈。

Method: 1）建立卫星负载下脉冲神经计算的数学形式化模型，推导能耗关于网络稀疏度、放电率与突触扇出的闭式表达式；2）提出三种算法：用于姿态估计的脉冲卡尔曼-互补滤波（SKCF）、用于航天器健康监测的时间对比异常检测器（TCAD）、用于认知卫星链路的脉冲自适应调制编码（SAMC），并给出伪代码与解析复杂度界；3）构建能量-精度-时延（EAL）权衡曲面，基于Loihi与SpiNNaker实测硅数据填充，与FPGA/GPU基线对比；4）细化四类抗辐射神经形态架构分类及故障率模型；5）以受约束STDP规则形式化在轨持续学习，并证明权重漂移有界。

Result: 在12U立方星对地观测、GEO通信载荷与自主深空探测器三个案例中给出量化设计点；神经形态实现相较GPU基线实现8–47倍能耗降低，典型卫星负载下推理精度保持在2.1%以内。

Conclusion: 神经形态计算在固定功率/质量/辐射预算下可为星载智能提供显著的能效优势，EAL曲面与抗辐射架构分类为工程选型提供量化依据，受约束STDP使在轨持续学习具备理论可证的稳定性。

Abstract: Satellite onboard computing faces an irreducible tension: mission intelligence requirements grow monotonically while power, mass, and radiation budgets remain fixed. This paper advances the state of the art on three fronts. First, we develop a formal mathematical model of spiking neural computation for satellite workloads, deriving closed-form expressions for energy consumption as a function of network sparsity, firing rate, and synaptic fan-out. Second, we propose three original algorithms -- a Spiking Kalman-Complementary Filter (SKCF) for attitude estimation, a Temporal Contrast Anomaly Detector (TCAD) for spacecraft health monitoring, and a Spike-Based Adaptive Modulation and Coding (SAMC) scheme for cognitive satellite links -- each with pseudocode and analytical complexity bounds. Third, we introduce a quantitative Energy-Accuracy-Latency (EAL) trade-off surface and populate it with numerical estimates derived from published silicon measurements of Loihi and SpiNNaker, enabling direct comparison with FPGA and GPU baselines. A taxonomy of four radiation-aware neuromorphic architecture classes is refined with fault-rate models. Continual on-orbit learning is formalized through a constrained Spike-Timing Dependent Plasticity (STDP) rule that provably bounds weight drift. Case studies for a 12U CubeSat Earth observation mission, a GEO communications payload, and an autonomous deep-space probe provide concrete, quantified design points. Our analysis shows that neuromorphic implementations can achieve 8 to 47 times energy reduction over GPU baselines while maintaining inference accuracy within 2.1\% for representative satellite workloads.

</details>


### [4] [Energy-Efficient Spiking Neural Networks for Sensing-Aided Beam Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12507)
*Nhan Thanh Nguyen,Phuong Nam Tran,Mengyuan Ma,Huy T. Nguyen,Ahmed Alkhateeb,Markku Juntti*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 该论文开发了用于波束预测的脉冲神经网络（SNN）架构，并从位置、视觉及多模态输入三个角度系统对比其与传统人工神经网络（ANN）的性能与能耗，揭示SNN在视觉/多模态感知下的能效优势及精度瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 感知辅助波束预测可利用视觉、位置等互补模态的环境信息降低毫米波系统的波束训练开销，但由此产生的感知与推理流程能耗较高，因此需要引入高能效的SNN作为传统ANN的替代方案。

Method: 构建面向波束预测的SNN架构，并在位置单独输入、视觉单独输入以及多模态输入三种设置下，与ANN就精度和能耗进行系统对比；同时分析SNN精度受限的成因。

Result: 在位置单独感知下，紧凑ANN持续优于SNN；而在视觉与多模态感知下，SNN在相近能耗下取得更高精度，并在相当精度下所需能耗低至一个数量级。例如多模态SNN达到58.08%的top-1精度，比能耗最接近的ANN高出18.11个百分点。此外，SNN精度受限于层间二值通信而非模型规模。

Conclusion: SNN在能效敏感的感知辅助波束预测中具有潜力，但存在精度局限性，其瓶颈主要来自层间二值通信。

Abstract: Sensing-aided beam prediction can reduce the beam-training overhead of millimeter-wave (mmWave) systems by leveraging environmental information from complementary modalities such as vision and position. However, the resulting sensing and inference pipeline can be energy-intensive, motivating the use of energy-efficient spiking neural networks (SNNs) as an alternative to conventional artificial neural networks (ANNs). In this work, we develop SNN architectures for beam prediction and systematically compare them with ANNs using position-only, vision-only, and multimodal inputs. Our results show that compact ANNs consistently outperform SNNs for position-only sensing. In contrast, for vision and multimodal sensing, SNNs achieve higher accuracy than ANNs at similar energy costs and require up to an order of magnitude less energy at a comparable accuracy. For example, the multimodal SNN achieves 58.08% top-1 accuracy, outperforming the closest-energy ANN by 18.11 percentage points. We further show that SNN accuracy is limited by the binary communication between layers rather than by model size. These results demonstrate the potential of SNNs for energy-efficient sensing-aided beam prediction while highlighting their accuracy limitation.

</details>


### [5] [REMBGO for Evaluation-Limited Optimization: CEC2022 Benchmarking and EAF-STATCOM Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13471)
*Vikram Chekuri,Narapareddy Ramarao,Prashanth N A*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出REMBGO——EMBGO在受限评估预算下的精炼版本，强调其增益来自多种机制的集成而非单一算子，并在CEC2022与STATCOM控制器整定中验证其"预算依赖"性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 元启发式算法的比较常在大评估预算下进行，远超昂贵工程仿真可承受的预算范围，导致结论与实际应用场景脱节。因此需要在贴近工程实际的受限函数评估预算下重新审视算法性能。

Method: 提出REMBGO：对EMBGO进行面向评估预算的精炼，整合种群保持、尺度归一化探索与恢复机制。在CEC2022上以1040次函数评估为主预算进行评估，并以预算阶梯描述性地刻画预算依赖性；开展组件移除研究并施加多重性校正；随后应用于EAF–STATCOM控制器整定，将冻结增益向量无重调地迁移到20个预设未见随机电弧实现（210次固定增益仿真）。

Result: 组件移除研究中没有任何单一机制的移除能通过多重性校正，支持"集成贡献"而非单算子贡献。所有210次固定增益仿真均可行。所选增益向量主效应仅占交叉验证变异的3.6%–5.8%，而交互加残差约占61.4%；REMBGO–EMBGO配对差异符号依控制器而定且统计不显著。

Conclusion: REMBGO应被表述为一种资源感知的精炼方法，其收益必须与评估预算一同陈述，而非普遍更优的优化器。

Abstract: Metaheuristic comparisons are often reported at evaluation budgets far larger than an expensive engineering simulation can afford. This paper presents REMBGO, an evaluation-budget-aware refinement of EMBGO. Its contribution is not any single operator: it is the integration of population preservation, scale-normalized exploration and recovery mechanisms whose parent-relative benefit is concentrated under restricted function-evaluation budgets. REMBGO is evaluated on CEC2022 at a primary, application-aligned budget of 1040 function evaluations, with a surrounding budget ladder reported descriptively to characterise budget dependence rather than as a family of controlled hypotheses. A component-removal study finds no individual mechanism whose removal survives multiplicity correction, consistent with an integrated rather than a single-operator contribution. The algorithm is then applied to EAF--STATCOM controller tuning, and the resulting frozen gain vectors are transferred without retuning to twenty pre-specified unseen stochastic arc realizations ($210$ fixed-gain simulations, all feasible). The selected gain-vector main effect accounts for only $3.6$--$5.8\%$ of the crossed validation variation, against approximately $61.4\%$ for interaction plus residual, and the matched REMBGO--EMBGO paired differences are controller-dependent in sign and not statistically significant. REMBGO is therefore presented as a resource-aware refinement whose benefit must be stated together with the evaluation budget, not as a universally superior optimizer.

</details>


### [6] [DyssomniaNN: Multi-Scale Spiking Neural Modeling of Human Sleep Disorders and Perceived Restedness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13918)
*Daniel Szelogowski*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: DyssomniaNN是一个多尺度生物物理计算框架，整合睡眠双过程调控、下丘脑互锁回路与皮层丘脑脉冲神经网络，模拟五类临床睡眠队列并复现脑电频谱，提出"休息充分指数"量化恢复效能，揭示睡眠时长无法预测恢复效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床睡眠医学中存在客观睡眠时长与主观日间功能损害之间的显著分离，如矛盾性失眠的睡眠状态误知觉和1型发作性睡病的边界不稳定，亟需机制性计算平台加以阐释。

Method: 耦合Borbély睡眠稳态与昼夜节律双过程、Saper下丘脑互锁翻转回路，以及递归皮层丘脑泄漏积分发放脉冲神经网络；在统一72小时光周期下形式化健康对照、昼夜节律睡眠-觉醒障碍（延迟相位）、慢性失眠、矛盾性失眠和1型发作性睡病五个队列。

Result: 合成局部场电位与PhysioNet Sleep-EDF经验脑电频谱高度吻合（各经典频段谱平均绝对误差0.33–0.35）；矛盾性失眠总睡眠时间正常（21.15 h）但恢复率仅37.6%，源于未受抑制的皮层β频段侵入；1型发作性睡病下丘脑分泌素缺乏导致严重夜间碎片化（入睡后觉醒781 min）及35次睡眠始发快速眼动期。

Conclusion: 睡眠时长不能预测恢复性恢复，DyssomniaNN为机制性睡眠研究和虚拟神经药理学提供了一体化在体计算平台。

Abstract: Clinical sleep medicine repeatedly documents profound divergences between objective sleep duration and subjective daytime impairment, notably exemplified by sleep state misperception in paradoxical insomnia and boundary instability in narcolepsy type 1. Here, we present DyssomniaNN: a multi-scale biophysical computational framework coupling Borbély's two-process regulation of sleep homeostasis and circadian drive with Saper's hypothalamic reciprocal flip-flop circuit and a recurrent corticothalamic leaky integrate-and-fire spiking neural network. We formalize five clinical cohorts across an identical 72-hour photoperiod: healthy control, circadian rhythm sleep-wake disorder (delayed-phase), chronic insomnia, paradoxical insomnia, and narcolepsy type 1. The synthetic local field potential converges closely with empirical human electroencephalography spectra from the PhysioNet Sleep-EDF database across all canonical frequency bands (spectral mean absolute error of 0.33--0.35). Furthermore, we introduce the Well-Rested Index to quantify restorative efficacy. Simulations reveal that sleep duration fails to predict restorative recovery: paradoxical insomnia achieves normal total sleep time (21.15 h) yet yields low restoration (37.6%) from unsuppressed cortical beta-band intrusion, whereas hypocretin deficiency in narcolepsy produces severe nocturnal fragmentation (781 min of wake after sleep onset) and 35 sleep-onset rapid eye movement periods. DyssomniaNN provides an in silico platform for mechanistic sleep research and virtual neuropharmacology.

</details>

### [7] [Environmental co-option of chromatin potential links prenatal hypoxia to persistent cortical circuit dysfunction](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.1101/2025.11.28.691133v2)
*Cristancho, A. G.,Joseph, D. J.,Cassidy, M. M.,Chauhan, P. S.,Gadra, E. J.,Gadra, E. C.,Zarrinnegar, D.,Rodriguez, B. N.,Marsh, E. D.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 产前瞬时缺氧引发皮层细胞类型特异性染色质重塑，谷氨酸能神经元染色质-转录偶联受损，导致幼年子代树突棘密度下降与兴奋性升高，提示缺氧通过"劫持"发育表观基因组编码未来转录状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 产前瞬时缺氧可导致长期神经发育障碍，但无明显脑结构损伤，说明早期调控紊乱先于可检测的病理改变；然而这些紊乱究竟是短暂应激反应，还是重编程了发育程序，尚不清楚。

Method: 在妊娠晚期小鼠皮层中，于瞬时缺氧后立即联合开展单核RNA测序（snRNA-seq）与染色质可及性图谱分析，覆盖超过14万个细胞核，比较不同细胞类型的转录与染色质响应。

Result: 缺氧在各细胞类型中诱导较为保守的转录应激反应，而染色质可及性改变更广泛且具细胞类型特异性；发育中的谷氨酸能神经元染色质-转录偶联受扰最严重，缺氧响应性远端调控区与突触组织和神经元兴奋性相关基因关联；与之一致，幼年子代皮层神经元树突棘密度降低、内在兴奋性升高。

Conclusion: 瞬时缺氧会"劫持"发育中表观基因组编码未来转录状态的能力，在持续性神经环路功能障碍出现之前即已埋下伏笔，支持表观遗传机制介导产前缺氧长期影响的模型。

Abstract: Transient prenatal hypoxia is associated with long-term neurodevelopmental impairment without overt structural brain injury, suggesting early regulatory disruptions precede detectable pathology. Whether these disruptions represent transient stress responses or redirect developmental programs remains unknown. Here, we combined single-nucleus RNA sequencing and chromatin accessibility profiling in the late-gestation mouse cortex immediately after transient hypoxia, capturing responses across more than 140,000 nuclei. Hypoxia induced a largely conserved transcriptional stress response across cell types, whereas chromatin accessibility showed more extensive, cell type-specific disruption. Developing glutamatergic neurons showed the strongest disruption of chromatin transcription coupling, with hypoxia-responsive distal regulatory regions associated with genes governing synaptic organization and neuronal excitability. Consistent with these chromatin-associated programs, juvenile offspring displayed reduced dendritic spine density and increased intrinsic excitability in cortical neurons. These results support a model in which transient hypoxia co-opts the developing epigenome's capacity to encode future transcriptional states before persistent circuit dysfunction emerges.

</details>


### [8] [Nicotinic modulation of frontal circuits breaks the cycle of attentional performance failure](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.10.05.756468v1)
*Nishioka, T.,Norman, K.,Garkun, Y.,Riceberg, J.,Waltrip, L.,Bicks, L.,Singh, A.,Serratelli, A.,Mankouskaya, T.,Maccario, P.,Allen, S.,Bateh, J.,Cho, C.,Caro, K.,Lopez, S.,Kawatake-Kuno, A.,Li, H.,Li, Y.,Wu, X.,O'loughlin, T.,Akbarian, S.,Morishita, H.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 研究揭示前额叶胆碱能机制通过α7-nAChR维持皮层环路活动，帮助在连续失败后恢复注意力与行为表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续失败后的恢复能力对适应性行为至关重要，但其神经机制尚不明确，作者希望找到支撑认知韧性的环路层面机制。

Method: 对额叶-感觉认知控制环路的神经调质输入进行在体筛选，结合胆碱能输入的必需/充分性操控、光遗传激活及受体水平分析。

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 本文提出"循环预测学习"(RPL)——一种受自监督学习启发的循环联合嵌入预测架构，将预测重点从输入空间转向表征空间，使神经回路能在无外部监督下从连续感觉流中学习物体身份与动态的抽象表征，并预测未来运动。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物生存依赖识别环境物体并预判其运动，大脑需从纠缠的感觉输入中解耦出物体身份与动态信息。经典预测编码理论虽能解释部分皮层计算，但主要聚焦于忠实预测感觉输入，未明确说明抽象、解耦的物体及其动态表征如何仅通过经验涌现。

Abstract: Whether tracking prey or evading a predator, an animal's survival depends on both identifying objects in the environment and anticipating how they will move. To guide behavior, the brain must therefore extract information about object identities and their dynamics from entangled sensory inputs. How it accomplishes this feat remains an open question. Classical predictive coding theories propose that this ability arises by comparing predicted sensory signals with actual inputs and reducing the associated prediction errors. While such models capture important aspects of cortical computation, they typically focus on faithfully predicting sensory input and do not explicitly address how abstract, untangled representations of objects and their dynamics emerge solely through experience. Here, we develop a theory of representation learning in neural circuits that shifts the focus from prediction in the input space to prediction in representation space, without relying on external supervision or labeled data. Specifically, we introduce recurrent predictive learning (RPL), a recurrent joint-embedding predictive architecture inspired by self-supervised learning, that learns abstract representations of object identity and their dynamics and predicts future object motion from continuous sensory streams. While local learning rules alone can learn invariant object identity, we find that capturing object dynamics requires a circuit that explicitly generates and minimizes internal prediction-error signals. Crucially, the model learns sequence representations that resemble successor-like representations observed in the human primary visual cortex. The model also develops abstract sequence representations comparable to those reported in the macaque prefrontal cortex. Finally, we demonstrate a hierarchical version of RPL and discuss possible mappings onto cortical microcircuits. Our work establishes a circuit-centric theory framework that provides new perspectives on how the brain may acquire an internal model of the world through experience.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Objective. Intermittent theta burst stimulation (iTBS) is a repetitive transcranial magnetic stimulation (rTMS) protocol used in clinical settings to alleviate symptoms of psychiatric disorders. Although the pulse parameters currently used in iTBS have remained unchanged since the first treatments, recent controllable TMS devices allow for the personalization of pulse shape and duration, opening the possibility of testing the efficacy of other pulse parameters in modulating corticospinal excitability (CSE). Methods. In a repeated-measures design, changes in CSE were induced by the administration of iTBS over the left primary motor cortex under three different conditions: long bidirectional pulse (L-Bi), long quasi-unidirectional pulse (L-QU), and a control short quasi-unidirectional pulse (S-QU). A mixed linear model showed that all conditions induced increased MEPs compared to baseline. However, the L-Bi pulse led to a larger and more sustained MEP increase than both unidirectional pulses, while the S-QU pulse appeared less effective in modulating CSE. We observed large within- and between-subject variability in responsiveness to the iTBS protocols. Conclusion. These results suggest that L-Bi pulses in iTBS protocols are more potent than unidirectional pulses in inducing CSE changes in the cortex of healthy adults. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原代皮层神经元培养广泛用于研究网络发育与退化，但其长期电生理轨迹尚缺乏系统刻画，尤其是网络衰竭的预测指标与机制尚不明确。

Method: 采用多电极阵列对Wistar大鼠皮层培养物进行最长约730 DIV的纵向记录；结合活动度指标、生存分析（含FDR校正的Cox比例风险模型，纳入13个候选特征）以及临界性指标（分支比、Kappa指数、神经元雪崩尺寸与持续时间指数及其标度关系）。

Result: 各孔共享早期发育期，平均放电率在DIV 100–150达峰值约12–14 Hz，随后分化为两种表型：早衰孔表现为爆发组织进行性丧失、爆发间期延长、爆发时长缩短及活跃电极招募陡降；长存活孔在DIV 400后乃至700以上仍维持有序爆发与可测活动。Cox模型识别出爆发时长、爆发间期、同步性、平均放电率、分支比与Kappa指数为网络衰竭时间的显著预测因子。临界性指标机制性最强：早衰孔中分支比趋近于零、Kappa指数接近上界；分支比与Kappa指数的变化速率是存活时间的最强相关因素。两种表型在活动期内雪崩指数及其标度关系均符合无标度动力学，早衰孔雪崩发生率进行性下降但未达独立预测显著性。

Conclusion: 长期网络存活由保留的爆发组织、持续的电极招募与近临界传播动力学决定，而非仅由放电率决定；基于临界性的指标是预测网络衰退与评估长期神经元功能保护干预的有前景的工具，该工作提供了迄今最长的啮齿类皮层培养电生理表征之一。

Abstract: Primary cortical neuron cultures are widely used to study network development and decline, but their long-term electrophysiological trajectories are poorly characterized. We used multi-electrode arrays to longitudinally monitor Wistar rat cortical cultures for up to ~730 days in vitro (DIV), combining activity measures with survival analysis and criticality metrics. Wells shared an early developmental phase, with mean firing rate peaking at 12-14 Hz around DIV 100-150, before diverging into two phenotypes: early-death wells, showing progressive loss of burst organization, longer inter-burst intervals, shorter bursts, and steep declines in active-electrode recruitment; and long-survival wells, which sustained organized bursting and measurable activity well beyond DIV 400, in some cases past DIV 700. Cox proportional-hazards modeling with FDR correction across thirteen candidate features identified burst duration, inter-burst interval, synchrony, mean firing rate, branching ratio, and Kappa index as significant predictors of time to network failure. Criticality metrics offered the most mechanistic insight: in early-death wells, branching ratio declined toward zero and Kappa index rose toward its upper bound as networks approached silencing. Beyond their average levels, the rate of change of branching ratio and Kappa index over recordings was the strongest correlate of survival time among all criticality metrics. Neuronal avalanche size and duration exponents and their scaling relation, remained consistent with scale-free dynamics in both phenotypes while networks were active, though avalanche occurrence rate declined progressively in early-death wells without reaching significance as an independent predictor. These results show that long-term network survival is not determined by firing rate alone, but by preserved burst organization, sustained electrode recruitment and near-critical propagation dynamics. This work offers one of the longest electrophysiological characterizations of rodent cortical cultures to date and identifies criticality-based metrics as promising tools for predicting network decline and evaluating interventions to preserve long-term neuronal function.

</details>

*Agnorelli, C.,Peill, J.,Kurtin, D. L.,Mallon, O.,Sawicka, G.,Shatalina, E.,Godfrey, K.,Murphy, R.,Ahmad, K.,Oestreich, L. K. M.,Rabiner, E.,Nutt, D.,Erritzoe, D.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 本研究结合[11C]-UCBJ PET、DWI/NODDI、rs-fMRI与EEG感觉任务，在18名健康志愿者中考察突触末梢SV2A密度与结构/功能神经可塑性指标的关系，并在氯胺酮(n=11)或DMT(n=7)药物挑战后检验其可重复性。结果显示SV2A结合电位(BPND)与ODI跨皮层区域耦合，且在视觉、注意及额顶控制网络中关联更紧密；与MMN重复抑制及晚期vLTP P2增强正相关，而与rs-fMRI功能加权度无关，为DWI和EEG指标作为人突触可塑性的替代指标提供了跨模态验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经可塑性是精神与神经退行性疾病的跨诊断特征及药物干预的假定作用机制。[11C]-UCBJ PET可定量突触囊泡糖蛋白2A(SV2A)密度，是人体突触密度最直接的在体测量手段，但其与更易扩展的结构与功能可塑性影像指标之间的关系尚未被表征，故需建立跨模态联系。

Method: 在18名健康志愿者中联合[11C]-UCBJ PET、弥散加权成像(DWI/NODDI)、静息态功能磁共振(rs-fMRI)及EEG感觉任务(roving错配负性MMN、视觉长时程增强vLTP)，检验突触末梢SV2A密度与互补结构与功能可塑性指标的关系，并在氯胺酮(n=11)或N,N-二甲基色胺(DMT;n=7)药物挑战后检验关系可重复性。

Result: DMT和氯胺酮暴露后1至8天，PET衍生的SV2A结合电位(BPND)、DWI衍生的神经突方向离散度(ODI)与结构加权度(SWD)、EEG衍生的MMN重复抑制及晚期vLTP P2增强均较基线无统计学显著差异。BPND与ODI在药物挑战前后均于皮层区域耦合，且在视觉、注意与额顶控制脑网络中关系更紧密；SWD与BPND关联较弱且空间受限(主要在边缘与视觉网络)，且给药后未复现；BPND与rs-fMRI功能加权度(FWD)无一致关联。额颞BPND与MMN重复抑制正相关(r=0.61)，枕叶BPND与晚期vLTP P2增强正相关(r=0.54)，其中MMN-BPND关联在药物挑战后复现(r=0.49)。

Conclusion: 这些发现将活体人脑突触末梢SV2A密度与神经突结构及短期和Hebbian样长时程突触可塑性的能力相联系，同时将其与泛化功能连接及感觉诱发反应幅度相区分。数据为DWI和EEG衍生指标作为人突触可塑性的生物学指数与替代测量提供了跨模态验证。

Abstract: Neuroplasticity, the brain's capacity to reorganise its structure and function, is a transdiagnostic feature across psychiatric and neurodegenerative diseases and a putative mechanism of action for pharmacological interventions. [11C]-UCBJ positron emission tomography (PET), which quantifies synaptic vesicle glycoprotein 2A (SV2A) density, offers the most direct in vivo measure of human synaptic density, but its relationship to complementary, more scalable neuroimaging measures of structural and functional plasticity remains uncharacterised. We combined [11C]-UCBJ PET, diffusion weighted imaging (DWI/NODDI), resting-state functional magnetic resonance imaging (rs-fMRI), and electroencephalography (EEG)-based sensory tasks (roving mismatch negativity, MMN; visual long-term potentiation, vLTP) in 18 healthy volunteers to test the relationship between synaptic terminal SV2A density and complementary structural and functional neuroplasticity measures, and whether these relationships were reproducible following pharmacological challenge with ketamine (n = 11) or N,N-dimethyltryptamine (DMT; n = 7). There were no statistically significant differences in PET-derived SV2A binding potential (BPND), DWI-derived neurite orientation dispersion (ODI) and structural weighted degree (SWD), EEG-derived MMN repetition suppression, and late-phase vLTP P2 potentiation, 1 to 8 days following DMT and ketamine exposure compared to baseline. BPND was coupled with ODI across cortical regions before and after pharmacological challenge, and this relationship was tighter within visual, attentional, and frontoparietal control brain networks. SWD showed a weaker, more spatially restricted association with BPND, primarily in regions belonging to the limbic and visual brain networks, but was not replicated post-drug. No consistent association was found between BPND and functional weighted degree (FWD), derived from rs-fMRI, at baseline or after pharmacological challenge. Frontotemporal BPND was positively associated with MMN repetition suppression (r = 0.61), and occipital BPND with late-phase vLTP P2 potentiation (r = 0.54), with the MMN-BPND association replicating after pharmacological challenge (r = 0.49). These findings link synaptic terminal SV2A density in the living human brain to neurite architecture and the capacity for short-term and Hebbian-like long-term synaptic plasticity, while dissociating these relationships from generic functional connectivity and magnitude of sensory-evoked responses. These data provide cross-modal validation for DWI- and EEG-derived measures as biological indices and surrogate measures of human synaptic plasticity.

</details>


### [14] [Adaptive co-transmission shapes sensory encoding and behavior](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.10.12.758587v1)
*Cuentas-Condori, A. A.,Kumar, S.,Agoba, P.,Chanaba-Lopez, P.,Diaz Garcia, M.,Colon-Ramos, D.*

TL;DR: 该研究发现线虫AFD温度感受神经元中谷氨酸能与胆碱能递质系统共存于同一突触前终末，但在轴突运输、亚突触组织和递质释放机制上存在差异，并受感觉经验差异性调控，分别以情境依赖方式参与趋近学习温度目标的行为控制，提出"适应性共传递"这一突触可塑性新形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共传递扩展了神经元的化学信号 repertoire，但共存的神经递质能否被独立组织、并被经验重塑以分别控制行为的不同方面，此前尚不清楚。

Method: 以秀丽隐杆线虫AFD温度感受神经元为模型，该神经元介导向学习温度偏好的导航行为。研究比较谷氨酸能（VGLUT/EAT-4）与胆碱能（VAChT/UNC-17）囊泡转运体的轴突运输、亚突触定位及对突触前机制的依赖性，并通过温度训练（偏好高温 vs 偏好低温）考察其表达变化，结合运动轨迹分析评估两种递质对导航及重定向决策的贡献。

Result: 两种囊泡转运体共存于单个突触前终末，但在轴突运输、亚突触组织和突触前机制依赖性上不同。经验差异性调控二者：训练偏好高温者VGLUT/EAT-4丰度增高，偏好低温者VAChT/UNC-17丰度增高。谷氨酸能传递促进向学习温度偏好移动，胆碱能传递则抑制其继续迁移越过目标；轨迹分析显示二者在温度梯度不同区域差异性调控重定向决策。

Conclusion: 研究揭示"适应性共传递"（adaptive co-transmission）——共存的神经递质系统被差异性组织并由经验调控的突触可塑性形式——并通过情境依赖的方式对行为做出不同贡献。

Abstract: Co-transmission expands the chemical repertoire of neurons, but whether coexisting neurotransmitters can be independently organized and remodeled by experience to control distinct aspects of behavior remains unclear. Here, we address this question in the AFD thermosensory neuron of Caenorhabditis elegans, which directs navigation toward a learned temperature preference. We find that glutamatergic and cholinergic vesicular transporters coexist within individual presynaptic boutons but differ in axonal trafficking, subsynaptic organization, and dependence on presynaptic machinery. Sensory experience differentially regulates the two systems: animals trained to prefer higher temperatures exhibit increased VGLUT/EAT-4 abundance, whereas those trained to prefer lower temperatures exhibit increased VAChT/UNC-17 abundance. The two transmitters also make distinct, context-dependent contributions to goal-directed navigation. Glutamatergic transmission promotes movement toward the learned thermal preference, whereas cholinergic transmission restrains continued migration beyond it. Analysis of locomotor trajectories further reveals that the two transmitters differentially regulate reorientation decisions across regions of the thermal gradient. Together, these findings identify adaptive co-transmission, a form of synaptic plasticity in which coexisting neurotransmitter systems are differentially organized and regulated by experience, and make distinct, context-dependent contributions to behavior.

</details>


### [15] [Scan field rotation with a K-mirror for fast two-photon imaging along curved planes](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.10.09.757907v1)
*Ghosh, S.,Godenzini, L.,Telliez, C.,Vladimirov, N.,Luechinger, L.,Liu, Y.,Helmchen, F.,Rupprecht, P.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 提出将K镜扫描场旋转与电可调透镜快速轴向扫描结合的模块化光学改造方案，实现对任意方向倾斜/弯曲成像面的快速聚焦跟踪，从而提升双光子成像对弯曲生物结构的成像效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物结构呈弯曲几何形状，而传统显微镜成像平面是平的。共振扫描显微镜中，轴向扫描速度可快于慢扫描轴（~30 Hz）但跟不上快共振轴（~8000 Hz），因此快速共振轴的固定方向限制了成像平面的倾斜或弯曲能力，使其难以与感兴趣的生物结构对齐。

Method: 将基于K镜的扫描场旋转与基于电可调透镜（ETL）的快速轴向扫描相结合，构成单一光学模块，可加装于标准双光子系统；并公开设计规格、3D打印部件和兼容ScanImage的自定义代码。

Result: 在小鼠海马中使成像平面与弯曲的锥体细胞层对齐，提高了同时成像神经元数量；成像了海马和皮层锥体神经元的树突分枝；应用于小脑浦肯野细胞的弯曲层；并通过扫描场旋转优化树突分枝方向以实现高速亚细胞谷氨酸成像。

Conclusion: 该方案克服了共振扫描显微镜中快轴方向固定对成像平面取向的限制，通用性强、可回溯改装、易于推广，为弯曲生物结构的快速体积/功能成像提供了灵活手段。

Abstract: In contrast to the curved geometry of biological structures, the imaging plane of conventional microscopes is flat. In principle, axial scanning can adjust the focus to follow curved structures. In resonant scanning microscopes, which are broadly used for video-rate functional imaging, however, such focus adjustment is typically possible at rates faster than the slow (~30 Hz) scan axis but not as fast as the fast (~8000 Hz) resonant axis. The fixed direction of the fast resonant axis therefore constrains how the imaging plane can be tilted or curved using axial scanning, and limits how well it can be aligned with biological structures of interest. Here, we present an optical modification that overcomes this constraint. Specifically, we combine scan field rotation using a K-mirror with fast axial scanning using an electrically tunable lens. We demonstrate the versatility of our approach for calcium imaging by aligning the imaging plane with the curved pyramidal cell layer in mouse hippocampus, increasing the number of simultaneously imaged neurons. In addition, we image dendritic arbors of hippocampal and cortical pyramidal neurons that would otherwise be only partially accessible for fast imaging, and we apply our method to the curved layers of cerebellar Purkinje cells. Furthermore, we demonstrate how scan field rotation can be used to optimally orient a dendritic branch for high-speed subcellular glutamate imaging. Our approach is based on a single module and can therefore be retrofitted to standard two-photon systems. We openly share design specifications, 3D-printed parts, and custom ScanImage-compatible code to facilitate rapid adoption.

</details>


### [16] [Trigeminal Nerve Stimulation Attenuates Traumatic Brain Injury-Induced Neuronal Damage via CGRP-PINK1 Mitophagy Axis](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.10.12.758678v1)
*Li, C.,Powell, K.,Wadolowski, S.,Kim, J.,Rams, K.,Tam, B.,Dehdashti, A.,Ullman, J. S.,LeDoux, D.,Becker, L. B.,Wang, P.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 非侵入性三叉神经刺激(TNS)通过激活内源性氧保存性潜水反射，促进神经元释放CGRP，抑制TOM20病理性聚集，恢复PINK1/Parkin线粒体质量控制通路功能，从而保护线粒体结构、减少细胞色素c泄漏与梗死扩大，改善创伤性脑损伤后的感觉运动功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 创伤性脑损伤后的继发性损伤由进行性线粒体功能障碍驱动，但目前临床上仍缺乏可直接保护线粒体网络的可行策略，构成重大治疗空白。本研究旨在探索一种非药物、非侵入性的神经调控手段来直接维护线粒体质量控制系统。

Method: 采用非侵入性三叉神经刺激(TNS)，激活脑内内源性氧保存性潜水反射；在TBI模型中考察TOM20聚集、PINK1结合与Parkin激活等线粒体外膜质量控制信号；分析CGRP依赖性机制、线粒体外膜屏障功能、细胞色素c泄漏、NRF2抗氧化防御以及梗死体积与感觉运动功能等指标。

Result: TBI以CGRP依赖方式诱导外膜受体TOM20病理性聚集，妨碍损伤感受器PINK1结合，使下游Parkin介导的修复失效；TNS诱导的神经元CGRP释放可阻止TOM20聚集，恢复PINK1功能与Parkin激活，维持线粒体结构、强化外膜屏障功能、限制促凋亡细胞色素c泄漏、支持NRF2抗氧化防御，最终减少进行性梗死扩大并恢复感觉运动功能。

Conclusion: 本研究将物理性神经调控与细胞器层面结构的保护直接关联，证明通过刺激三叉神经传入以招募氧保存反射，可稳定线粒体外膜信号架构并保存线粒体存活机制，为保护线粒体功能、抑制急性创伤后神经退行性变提供了一种非药物治疗策略。

Abstract: Secondary damage after traumatic brain injury (TBI) is driven by progressive mitochondrial failure, yet clinically viable strategies to directly safeguard mitochondrial networks remain a major therapeutic gap. Here, we show that non-invasive trigeminal nerve stimulation (TNS), which engages the brain's endogenous oxygen-conserving diving reflex, preserves neuronal survival by directly regulating the mitochondrial PINK1/Parkin quality-control pathway. We demonstrate that TBI induces the pathological aggregation of the outer mitochondrial membrane receptor TOM20, in a CGRP-dependent manner, which affects binding of the damage-sensor PINK1 and renders downstream Parkin-mediated rescue ineffective. TNS-induced neuronal CGRP release prevents this TOM20 aggregation, restoring proper PINK1 function and downstream Parkin activation. This preservation of mitochondrial architecture reinforces the outer membrane's barrier function, limiting pro-apoptotic cytochrome c leakage and supporting NRF2 antioxidant defenses, ultimately reducing progressive infarct expansion and recovering sensorimotor functions. The significance of this work lies in bridging physical neuromodulation directly to the preservation of organelle-level architecture. By demonstrating that stimulation of trigeminal afferents to recruit an oxygen-conserving reflex can stabilize outer membrane signaling architecture and preserve mitochondrial survival machinery, this study outlines a non-pharmacological strategy to protect mitochondrial function and inhibit acute post-traumatic neurodegeneration.

</details>
