Abstract: Coding agents often make useful progress before they fail, but retry-from-scratch discards the machine state created along the way. This makes failure recovery an interesting search problem: if intermediate environment states preserve meaningful progress, restoring and branching from them could outperform repeatedly restarting from the initial state. We implement Go-Explore-style search over full Daytona snapshots and find that higher and more efficient solve rates depend mainly on progress that sits outside the repository, such as databases, services, and other runtime state. Our results suggest that full-snapshot search is most effective when long-horizon coding work spans multiple services whose state cannot be reconstructed from a single diff.

</details>


### [156] [Inference-Time Steering Across Scaffolds and Epitopes for Sample-Efficient De Novo Nanobody Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13844)
*Mason Minot,Simon Friedensohn,Cédric R. Weber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将生成式分子设计中的条件选择重构为推理期资源分配问题，提出Global SMC与BaSE两种动态预算分配器，在保持多样性的同时将可设计候选产出效率提升近5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式分子设计通常依赖过度生成加事后过滤，效率低。Feynman-Kac（FK）steering等推理期引导方法虽能提高效率，但只在单一固定配置下优化。在de novo纳米抗体设计中，需预先确定scaffold identity或target epitope等条件，而不同条件下产出率差异大，对前景不佳的配置继续引导会浪费算力，因此需要一种跨配置的动态计算资源分配机制。

Method: 将生成式条件选择重新表述为推理期资源分配问题，提出两种动态预算分配器：（1）联合的Global Sequential Monte Carlo（SMC）表述，在多个配置间同时进行引导与计算分配；（2）解耦方法，采用改进的Batched Successive Elimination（BaSE）。在de novo纳米抗体设计场景下，针对多个不同治疗靶点与生成配置进行实验验证。

Result: 在多个不同治疗靶点和生成配置下，两种动态分配器将每GPU小时可设计候选的产出率提升近5倍，且不牺牲候选多样性。

Conclusion: 动态预算分配可有效缓解纳米抗体从头设计中条件预设带来的算力瓶颈，在不损失多样性的前提下显著提升生成效率，为推理期引导的跨配置资源分配提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Generative molecular design typically relies on over-generation and post-hoc filtering. Inference-time steering methods like Feynman-Kac (FK) steering improve efficiency by biasing trajectories toward high-reward candidates, yet they optimize within a single fixed configuration. In de novo nanobody design, upfront commitment to conditioning choices like scaffold identity or target epitope creates a bottleneck. Since yield varies across these conditions, steering unpromising setups wastes compute. We recast generative condition selection as an inference-time resource-allocation problem. We propose two dynamic budget allocators: a joint Global Sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) formulation that steers and allocates compute across configurations concurrently, and a decoupled approach adapting Batched Successive Elimination (BaSE). Empirically, these dynamic allocators increase the yield of designable candidates per GPU-hour nearly 5$\times$ without sacrificing diversity across distinct therapeutic targets and generative configurations.

</details>


### [157] [Partially Informed Stochastic Optimization via Zeroth-Order Residual Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13871)
*El Mahdi Chayti,Alireza Mirrokni,Martin Jaggi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PISO结合可用梯度分量与残差运行估计，将零阶探测的方差从残差幅度转为跟踪误差，实现随机草图迭代并给出收敛保证及更优函数评估复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多优化问题只能直接访问部分梯度，剩余梯度需通过函数评估推断，如performative prediction、performative reinforcement learning和黑盒下层bilevel optimization。现有方法难以处理不可用梯度分量且往往需均匀界假设。

Method: 提出Partially Informed Stochastic Optimization (PISO)：将已知梯度分量与残差的running estimate结合，用该估计中心化每次零阶观测，使随机探测方差依赖跟踪误差而非残差大小，形成带显式收缩机制的随机草图迭代并给出动量解释。

Result: 对光滑非凸目标建立依赖残差变化与初始幅度的收敛保证，无需不可用梯度分量的均匀界；在独立函数评估及高斯或球面探测下，Lipschitz梯度时达到O(d^2ε^-6)，附加Lipschitz-Hessian假设时达到O(d^2ε^-5)，每次更新仅需一个探测方向、两个函数样本和一个已知分量样本；分析还量化了批处理对观测与部署的权衡。五个设定中，PISO在三个中优于基线，在bilevel routing中更快达到可比解，在无响应模型导数的performative RL中表现最佳。

Conclusion: PISO通过中心化零阶观测和残差跟踪有效降低探测方差，减少对不可用梯度分量均匀界的依赖，获得领先的函数评估复杂度并在多个任务上优于基线。

Abstract: Many optimization problems provide direct access to only part of the gradient, while the remaining component must be inferred from function evaluations. This structure arises in performative prediction, performative reinforcement learning, and bilevel optimization with a black-box lower level. We introduce Partially Informed Stochastic Optimization (PISO), which combines the available gradient component with a running estimate of the residual. By centring each zeroth-order observation on this estimate, PISO makes the variance due to random probing depend on the tracking error rather than the residual's magnitude. This yields a randomized sketch iteration with an explicit contraction mechanism and a principled interpretation of momentum. For smooth nonconvex objectives, we establish convergence guarantees that depend on the residual's variation and initial magnitude, without requiring a uniform bound on the unavailable gradient component. With independently sampled function evaluations and Gaussian or spherical probes, PISO attains leading function-evaluation complexities of \(O(d^{2}\varepsilon^{-6})\) under Lipschitz gradients and \(O(d^{2}\varepsilon^{-5})\) under an additional Lipschitz-Hessian assumption. These rates require only one probe direction, two function samples, and one known-component sample per update. The analysis also quantifies how batching trades observations for fewer deployments. Across five settings, PISO variants improve on the tested baselines with comparable oracle access in three, reach a comparable solution faster in bilevel routing, and perform best among methods without response-model derivatives in performative reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [158] [Prediction Is Not a Sufficient Memory State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13881)
*Robert Jenkinson Alvarez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对精确累积学习与注意力机制下的"记忆-学习"区分进行了刻画：对光滑凸残差损失（无约束实数特征与标签），固定维度的连续递归状态存在当且仅当损失为多项式；次数为 D 时所需状态维度恰为 r=binom(d+D,D)-1。非多项式损失在"相同当前预测"的历史上需求无界，岭回归与核注意力分别给出了第二个精确闭包分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文动机在于指出一个被忽视的区分：一个能复现当前所有预测的记忆，可能仍已"遗忘如何从下一次观测中学习"。作者希望从理论上刻画"仅保存当前预测"与"精确累积学习"之间的根本差异，说明未来更新究竟揭示了多少隐藏信息，而不是提出新的回归算法或宣称训练模型性能提升。

Method: 方法上，作者针对光滑凸残差损失、无约束实数特征与标签的设定，研究固定维度连续递归状态的存在性与最小维度需求；在普通无权观测下推导次数 D 所需坐标数 r=binom(d+D,D)-1，并考察在固定当前预测的历史子集上仍需 r-d 个坐标的情形。进一步分析非多项式损失、有界标量 logistic 例子（固定任意有限阶导数 jet 后仍保持该障碍）、岭回归（精确状态需 dp+d(d+1)/2 维，而仅存预测需 dp 维）以及核注意力，给出两个精确闭包分类，并对良态零预测族分析最优 B 比特压缩的平均单步更新误差。

Result: 结果显示：多项式损失才允许有限维连续递归状态精确实现累积学习，且维度为 binom(d+D,D)-1；非多项式损失在等预测历史上需求无界。岭回归中隐藏证据即设计矩阵，精确状态成本为 dp+d(d+1)/2，仅存预测为 dp。在良态零预测族上，即使允许任意随机化编码，最优 B 比特压缩的平均单步更新误差阶为 d^{-1}2^{-2B/s_d}，其中 s_d=d(d+1)/2。logistic 例子在固定任意有限导数 jet 后仍维持该障碍。核注意力给出第二个精确闭包分类。

Conclusion: 结论指出，复现当前预测并不等价于保留继续学习的能力，二者之间存在可精确量化的维度与压缩代价差异。本文的贡献在于刻画未来更新所揭示的信息与所需的精确状态规模，而非提出新的回归算法或断言训练模型的改进。

Abstract: A memory that reproduces every current prediction may still have forgotten how to learn from the next observation. We characterize this distinction for exact cumulative learning and attention. For smooth convex residual losses with unrestricted real features and labels, a continuous recurrent state of fixed dimension exists exactly when the loss is polynomial. With ordinary, unweighted observations, degree $D$ requires precisely $r=\binom{d+D}{D}-1$ coordinates; even among histories with one fixed current predictor, exactly $r-d$ coordinates can remain necessary for continuation. Nonpolynomial losses have unbounded requirements on such equal-prediction histories. Bounded scalar logistic examples retain this obstruction after fixing any finite derivative jet. For ridge regression, the hidden evidence is the design matrix: the exact state costs $dp+d(d+1)/2$, versus $dp$ for the predictor. On a well-conditioned zero-prediction family, optimal $B$-bit compression has average one-update error of order $d^{-1}2^{-2B/s_d}$, $s_d=d(d+1)/2$, even with arbitrary randomized codes. Kernel attention gives a second exact closure classification. The results identify what future updates reveal, rather than proposing a new regression algorithm or asserting trained-model gains.

</details>


### [159] [Revisiting Tabular Deep Regression: Objectives and the Limits of Global Early Stopping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13909)
*Danil Rudenko,Georgii Bulgakov,Nikolay Kartashev,Ivan Rubachev,Daniil Krasylnikov,Artem Babenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对表格数据回归任务的目标函数进行系统重评估，发现分位数损失在多种神经骨干网络上稳定优于调优后的MSE基线，并指出不同噪声区域最优停止轮次不一致会削弱全局早停效果，据此提出异方差感知的分位数损失扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格深度学习的回归目标函数选择长期缺乏系统研究。已有替代MSE的方案因数据集、骨干网络与评估协议不统一，难以公平比较其实际收益。

Method: 在大量经典表格数据集和多种神经骨干网络上对回归目标函数进行统一的系统复评；并在分位数损失基础上引入输入依赖的标签平滑，构建异方差感知的目标函数扩展。

Result: 多数提出的目标函数相较充分调参的MSE基线收益有限或不稳定；分位数损失在各骨干网络上取得一致的总体提升；但不同目标噪声区域的最优训练轮次不同，单一全局早停检查点会在不确定区域之间造成折衷。

Conclusion: 研究给出了表格深度学习回归目标函数的现代实证图景，说明跨不确定区域最优停止时间的差异会限制全局早停的有效性；所提出的异方差感知分位数损失在多个骨干网络上优于分位数损失，代码已开源。

Abstract: While deep learning models for tabular data have recently received significant research attention, the choice of objective functions for regression tasks remains an understudied degree of freedom. Several lines of research offer alternatives to the standard Mean Squared Error (MSE), but differences in datasets, backbones, and evaluation protocols make their practical benefits difficult to compare. In this work, we address this gap by performing a systematic re-evaluation of regression objectives across a broad collection of established tabular datasets and multiple neural backbones. Our comparison reveals that many proposed objectives provide limited or inconsistent gains over a properly tuned MSE baseline, while quantile loss yields consistent aggregate improvements over MSE across the tested backbones. We then identify a limitation that remains even with this strong objective: regions with different levels of target noise can reach their best predictive performance at different training epochs. Consequently, a single global early-stopping checkpoint can impose a compromise between uncertainty regions, even when the objective can represent heteroskedastic target distributions. Motivated by this limitation, we introduce a heteroskedasticity-aware extension of quantile loss that uses input-dependent label smoothing as a potential remedy and improves over quantile loss on several backbones. Overall, our results provide a modern empirical picture of regression objectives for tabular deep learning and highlight how differences in optimal stopping times across uncertainty regions can limit the effectiveness of global early stopping. The code is available at https://github.com/yandex-research/tabular-regression.

</details>


### [160] [Designing Protein Structures from Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13917)
*Joseph Lee,Yanjing Li,Bo Qiang,Gregory Lau,Allyson Ettinger,Noah A. Smith,Rory Donovan-Maiye,David Baker,Hanna Hajishirzi,Pang Wei Koh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Prompteina，首个支持自然语言指定设计目标的全原子蛋白质结构生成模型。通过构建 Prompteina-Data（180万结构、5000万+文本描述）并对预训练生成模型 Proteina-Complexa 进行文本条件预训练与微调，模型在18项结构属性中15项超越基线，在binder设计中20个靶点中至少13个领先，界面置信度提升14%，并针对无先例靶点 GM2A 实验验证获得9个binder，最强表观 K_D 达107 nM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 de novo 蛋白质设计模型通常需要为每种属性设计专门的输入表示，限制了可支持的设计目标范围。作者希望像文本到图像生成一样，用自然语言作为统一且灵活的蛋白质设计条件接口。

Method: 1) 构建 Prompteina-Data：包含180万蛋白质结构和5000万+描述全局及残基特异结构功能属性的配对文本-结构数据集；2) 基于预训练结构生成模型 Proteina-Complexa，采用文本条件预训练与微调，将残基与其对应文本对齐；3) 结合迭代 in silico 反馈进行设计优化。

Result: 在18项结构属性中15项优于基线，生成蛋白更符合目标功能描述；binder设计在20个靶点中至少13个领先，界面置信度比最强基线提升14%；在最难靶点上迭代相对非迭代的增益比次优基线大57%；对无已报道 de novo binder 的靶点 GM2A 实验验证识别出9个binder，最强表观 K_D 为107 nM。

Conclusion: 自然语言可作为蛋白质结构生成的有效且强大的条件接口，支持更广泛的设计目标，并具备实验验证的实用性。

Abstract: A central challenge in de novo protein design is generating proteins with desired structural and functional properties. Existing design models typically require a dedicated input representation for each property, limiting the design objectives they support. We develop Prompteina, the first all-atom structure generation model that supports new and broader design objectives specified in natural language, analogous to text-to-image generation. To train Prompteina, we construct Prompteina-Data, a paired text--protein structure dataset of 1.8M structures and 50M+ captions describing global and residue-specific structural and functional properties. We adapt Proteina-Complexa, a pretrained structure generation model, through text-conditioned pretraining and finetuning that aligns residues with their corresponding text. Prompteina outperforms baselines on 15 of 18 structural properties and generates proteins that more closely match descriptions of their intended functions. In binder design, it leads baselines on at least 13 of 20 targets and improves interface confidence by 14\% over the strongest baseline. With iterative in silico feedback, Prompteina's gain over non-iterative generation is 57\% larger than the next-best baseline on the hardest targets. Finally, we experimentally validate Prompteina designs against GM2A, a target with no reported de novo binders. We identify nine binders, with the strongest at an apparent $K_{\mathrm{D}}$ of 107 nM. These results establish natural language as an effective and powerful conditioning interface for protein structure generation.

</details>


### [161] [KL Before and After Mixing: A Round-Complexity Separation in Parallel Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13924)
*Tianyi Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了并行采样器的两种KL散度准则差异：调度平均散度 $\mathbb{E}_U D_{\mathrm{KL}}(p\|q_U)$ 与输出律散度 $D_{\mathrm{KL}}(p\|\mathbb{E}_U q_U)$。对均匀二进制定权目标，前者最优期望轮数为 $\Theta_\varepsilon((\log n)^2)$，后者为 $(e^{-\varepsilon}+o(1))\log_2\ln n$，呈现显著分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行采样器可随机选择坐标联合生成，但其输出律依赖于随机调度。作者希望厘清：按调度平均衡量散度，与按输出律的混合分布衡量散度，两种准则下最优采样成本是否存在本质差异，以及这种差异量级如何。

Method: 将每轮锁定在轮始的精确条件边际独立抽样，每个输出的调度在有限调度混合中先抽取后采样，并计费每一轮。针对偶数长 $n$、含 $n/2$ 个 1 的二进制均匀目标，构造计数状态递归，给出在所有因果随机策略下每轮数上限的精确最小输出散度，并推广到加权定权目标及 Beta$(1,1)$–Bernoulli 全支撑目标。

Result: 对均约定权目标，固定容差 $\varepsilon$ 下：调度平均准则最小期望轮数为 $\Theta_\varepsilon((\log n)^2)$；输出律准则为 $(e^{-\varepsilon}+o(1))\log_2\ln n$，且双对数阶可在每次运行同一轮数下达到。计数状态递归给出精确最小输出散度。分离可推广至有界权重比与配额比例的加权定权目标、无序对称情形，以及全支撑 Beta$(1,1)$–Bernoulli 目标，其锐期望成本为 $(e^{-\varepsilon}/2+o(1))\log_2\log_2 n$，由稀有 $n$ 轮运行达到。先前工作已给出调度平均成本的阶及两个扩展上的 $O_\varepsilon((\log n)^2)$ 上界。

Conclusion: 两种散度准则导致最优期望采样成本出现从 $\Theta((\log n)^2)$ 到 $\Theta(\log\log n)$ 的指数级分离，说明衡量输出分布的方式对并行采样复杂度的刻画至关重要；该分离在若干目标类中稳健成立，并通过精确递归刻画了因果随机策略的最优性。

Abstract: A parallel sampler can choose at random which coordinates to generate together. For a target $p$ and the output law $q_U$ of a schedule $U$ drawn afresh for each output, we compare the schedule-averaged divergence $\mathbb{E}_U D_{\mathrm{KL}}(p\|q_U)$ with the output-law divergence $D_{\mathrm{KL}}(p\|\mathbb{E}_U q_U)$, in nats. Rounds commit independent draws from exact conditional marginals frozen at the round's start, each output's schedule is drawn before sampling from a finite mixture of schedules, and every scheduled round is charged. For the uniform distribution on binary strings of even length $n$ with $n/2$ ones, the least expected number of rounds at a fixed tolerance $\varepsilon>0$ is $Θ_\varepsilon((\log n)^2)$ under the first criterion but $(e^{-\varepsilon}+o(1))\log_2\ln n$ under the second, and the double-logarithmic order is attainable with the same round count on every run. For this target, a count-state recursion gives the exact least output divergence at every round cap over all causal randomized policies. The separation extends to weighted fixed-count targets with bounded weight ratios and quota fractions bounded away from zero and one, without permutation symmetry, and to the full-support Beta$(1,1)$--Bernoulli target, where the sharp expected cost under the output-law criterion is $(e^{-\varepsilon}/2+o(1))\log_2\log_2n$, attained with rare $n$-round runs. Prior work implies the order of the schedule-averaged cost on the balanced target and its $O_\varepsilon((\log n)^2)$ upper bounds on both extensions.

</details>


### [162] [AI Models Can Provably Hide Arbitrary Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13928)
*Andis Draguns,Sumeet Ramesh Motwani,Raymond Douglas,Christian Schroeder de Witt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文构建基于加密神经网络的后门攻击，证明在对抗条件下隐藏能力无法被诱导，揭示了基于诱导的能力审计的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能力评估旨在部署前发现危险模型行为，但其可靠性依赖于"隐藏能力可被诱导"这一假设。作者希望检验该假设在对抗条件下是否成立，以评估诱导式能力审计的有效性边界。

Method: 利用数字储物柜（digital lockers）构造后门攻击，将加密神经网络嵌入宿主模型权重中，仅在收到秘密触发器时解密并执行。在PyTorch中实现概念验证，并在小型化学反应预测任务（CBRN相关能力的代理任务）上，通过微调、权重加噪、基于梯度的触发器搜索和潜在对抗训练进行诱导测试。

Result: 在加密假设下，即使拥有完整的模型权重访问权限，隐藏电路仍可证明难以被诱导和解释；实验显示对上述多种诱导方法均具抵抗力。该工作将先前隐藏固定字符串的研究扩展到任意计算。

Conclusion: 结果与密码学保证共同表明，基于诱导的能力审计存在根本性局限。作者发布模型以支持防御方法的改进。

Abstract: Capability evaluations aim to surface dangerous model behaviors before deployment, but their reliability depends on the assumption that hidden capabilities can be elicited. We show this assumption does not hold under adversarial conditions by constructing backdoor attacks that embed encrypted neural networks within host model weights, decrypting and executing them only upon receiving a secret trigger. Using digital lockers, these hidden circuits remain provably hard to elicit and interpret under cryptographic assumptions, even given full access to model weights, extending prior work from hiding fixed strings to arbitrary computations. We implement our proof of concept constructions in PyTorch and evaluate elicitation through fine-tuning, weight noising, gradient-based trigger search, and latent adversarial training on a small chemical reaction prediction task - a proxy for CBRN-relevant capabilities. Our results demonstrate resistance to these methods and, together with our cryptographic guarantees, reveal a limit of elicitation-based capability audits. We release our models to support the improvement of defenses.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [163] [FlyHack: Stress-Testing Mechanistic Claims in a Connectome-Constrained Visual Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13018)
*Aashish Dhawan*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 该论文对连接组约束的果蝇运动视觉模型 flyvis 进行了系统性压力测试，发现其行为与机制结论高度依赖比较模型、干预方式、电路表示及训练解，因此此类模型的推论须限定在具体条件范围内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连接组约束神经网络使生物组件可直接接受干预，但其机制结论仍可能依赖比较模型、干预手段、电路表示与训练所得解，作者希望检验这些依赖性的稳健程度。

Method: 利用 flyvis（果蝇运动视觉模型）的 50 模型集成、任务匹配的因果 CNN 对照以及可复制的干预实验进行压力测试；行为层面考察反相关（reverse-phi）反转、静止锯齿波响应与对抗性方向反转的扰动边界；机制层面比较一阶边缘归因修补（含零对齐评分）的 AUROC 与 Spearman 相关，并分析贪心细胞类型集合、T4/T5 调谐判据、输出沉默以及连接集剪枝结果。

Result: 行为上，flyvis 的三级反相关反转强烈依赖周期与速度，并同样出现在任务匹配 CNN 中，二者均未复现果蝇的静止锯齿波响应；对抗性方向反转在 flyvis 中需 0.21--0.27 倍刺激对比度的扰动界限，CNN 为 0.13--0.25，但在参数匹配 CNN 上差距随规模增大而基本消失。机制上，归因与干预的匹配至关重要：留一均值替换下，一阶边缘归因修补达 AUROC 0.970、Spearman 0.872，而零对齐评分仅 0.933 与 0.126。电路结论随表示与集成成员变化：50 个模型中 25 个满足模型相对的 T4/T5 调谐判据，T4/T5 输出沉默仅在 8 段片段中的至多 2 段降低所测光流行为；6 个首次成功模型中连接集可剪枝至新增 13--31 类，但在双侧响应幅度检验下仅 2 个仍满足调谐阈值。

Conclusion: 连接组约束模型使机制假设可直接检验，但结论须限定于特定模型家族、干预、表示与训练解，应支持更窄范围的使用。

Abstract: Connectome-constrained neural networks make biological components directly accessible to intervention, but their mechanistic conclusions can still depend on the comparison model, intervention, circuit representation and trained solution. We stress-test these dependencies in flyvis, a \emph{Drosophila} motion-vision model, using its 50-model ensemble, task-matched causal CNN controls and replicated interventions. At the level of model behaviour, three-level reverse-phi reversal in flyvis is strongly period- and velocity-dependent and also appears in task-matched CNNs; neither family reproduces the fly's stationary-sawtooth response. Adversarial direction reversal requires a perturbation bound of 0.21--0.27 times stimulus contrast in flyvis versus 0.13--0.25 in CNNs, but the gap over a parameter-matched CNN largely closes for larger CNNs. At the level of mechanism, matching attribution to the intervention matters: for held-out-mean replacement, first-order edge attribution patching reaches pooled AUROC 0.970 and Spearman 0.872, versus 0.933 and 0.126 when its zero-aligned score is evaluated against the same ground truth. Circuit conclusions also vary with representation and ensemble member. Greedy cell-type sets meet a model-relative T4/T5 tuning criterion in 25 of 50 models, while T4/T5 output silencing reduces the tested optic-flow behaviour to at most two of eight clips in 40. In six models where the cell-type search first succeeds, the corresponding connection sets can be pruned to 13--31 added classes; under an additional two-sided response-amplitude check, only two of those six first-success cell-type sets still meet the tuning threshold. These results support a narrower use of connectome-constrained models: they make mechanistic hypotheses directly testable, but the resulting claims must be scoped to the model family, intervention, representation and trained solution.

</details>


### [164] [Excitability across scales: a unifying EI model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13275)
*Thomas S. J. Burger,Rodolphe Sepulchre*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 提出时空兴奋-抑制（EI）模型，用混合反馈架构统一细胞与群体尺度的神经元兴奋性建模，再现群体放电与节律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经元兴奋性在细胞尺度与群体尺度常采用不同建模原理，导致难以研究微观机制如何塑造宏观活动。

Method: 构建时空EI模型：保留Hodgkin-Huxley类电导电路结构，用忆导（memductance）描述本征与突触电流，其时空电压依赖性编码相应尺度动力学。最小形式中，时间兴奋性来自快速兴奋反馈对抗慢速抑制反馈；空间兴奋性来自短程兴奋对抗长程抑制，对应Amari神经场的侧抑制模体。

Result: 在强异质群体中产生群体兴奋性，生成时空局部化的群体棘波，其群体尺度不同于单个神经元棘波，且在显著细胞间参数变异下依然稳健；本征时间兴奋性与空间突触反馈对相干平均场事件和稳健时空群体节律均必不可少；选择性调节部分群体的忆导可调谐群体节律频率。

Conclusion: 通过将电路架构与建模尺度分离，该框架为跨尺度分析、设计神经元兴奋性提供了物理可解释的方法。

Abstract: Neuronal excitability is commonly modeled using different principles at the cellular and population scales, making it difficult to study how microscopic mechanisms shape macroscopic activity. We introduce a spatiotemporal excitatory-inhibitory (EI) model that unifies these scales through a common mixed-feedback architecture. The model retains the conductance-based circuit structure of Hodgkin-Huxley models and describes intrinsic and synaptic currents through memductances whose temporal and spatial voltage dependence encodes dynamics at the relevant scale. In its minimal form, temporal excitability arises from fast excitatory feedback opposed by slower inhibitory feedback, while spatial excitability arises from short-range excitation opposed by longer-range inhibition, recovering the core lateral-inhibition motif of Amari neural fields. We show that combining these mechanisms gives rise to population excitability in strongly heterogeneous populations. In particular, the model generates population spikes that are localized in both space and time and possess population-level scales distinct from those of individual neuronal spikes, despite substantial cell-to-cell parameter variability. We then show that both intrinsic temporal excitability and spatial synaptic feedback are required for coherent mean-field events and robust spatiotemporal population rhythms. Finally, selective modulation of memductances in a subset of the population tunes the frequency of the population rhythm, illustrating how changes at the cellular scale can shape macroscopic dynamics. By separating circuit architecture from modeling scale, the proposed framework provides a physically interpretable approach to analyzing and designing neuronal excitability across scales.

</details>


### [165] [Beyond Anxiety and Avoidance: A Dynamic Feedback-Regulation Framework for Adult Romantic Attachment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13312)
*Mateusz Pomianek*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 该文提出"依恋调节与控制"(ARC)框架,将成人依恋焦虑与回避作为调节变量,整合控制论取向的反馈调节、安全基地表征、感知伴侣回应性、关系差异等要素,构建成人浪漫关系中的时序性反馈-调节序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成人依恋通常以焦虑和回避两个个体层面维度表征,但这些维度本身无法说明个体如何在具体情境中感知关系状态、与期望状态比较、转化为行动并依据人际反馈进行更新,因而缺乏对时间性过程机制的解释。

Method: 理论整合与模型构建。ARC整合已有控制论依恋观点、安全基地表征、感知伴侣回应性、关系差异、二元调节与恢复,提出七步时序序列:感知关系状态→期望关系状态→差异→调节行动→伴侣反馈→状态更新→恢复。并将差异分数视为可证伪的建模约束,与"仅感知状态"、"感知+期望的非约束模型"及"非对称差异"备择模型进行比较。

Result: ARC将依恋焦虑与回避定位为核心调节变量;安全基地表征作为需检验其增量预测力的候选变量;心智化情感性与调节灵活性设为可选调节变量;成人依恋紊乱则作为独立的延伸结果变量。

Conclusion: ARC为成人浪漫依恋提供了一个明确的、可实证检验的反馈调节框架,其核心贡献在于将时间性与过程性假设显性化,并通过模型比较使差异分数等关键设定具备可证伪性。

Abstract: Adult attachment is commonly represented by attachment anxiety and avoidance, but these person-level dimensions do not by themselves specify how momentary relational conditions are perceived, compared with desired conditions, translated into action, and updated after interpersonal feedback. We present the Attachment Regulation and Control (ARC) framework as an integrative feedback-regulation account that makes these temporal assumptions explicit and empirically testable. In ARC, control refers to feedback-based regulation toward relational goals in the control-theoretic sense, not interpersonal dominance, coercion, or a generalized need for control. ARC does not claim a novel control-system architecture. Instead, it synthesizes established control-theoretic attachment ideas, secure-base representations, perceived partner responsiveness, relational discrepancy, dyadic regulation, and recovery into a parsimonious sequence for adult romantic relationships: perceived relational state, desired relational state, discrepancy, regulatory action, partner feedback, state updating, and recovery. The framework treats the discrepancy score itself as a falsifiable modeling restriction by comparing it with perceived-state-only, unconstrained perceived-plus-desired-state, and asymmetric discrepancy alternatives. Attachment anxiety and avoidance form core regulation moderators. Secure-base representation is a candidate requiring incremental prediction of recovery outcomes. Mentalized affectivity and regulation flexibility remain optional moderators, while adult disorganization is an independent extension outcome.

</details>


### [166] [EEG Entrainment to Non-Speech Rhythmic Patterns Associated with Dimensions of Language Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13419)
*Francisco J. Martínez-Murcia,Nicolás J. Gallego-Molina,Marco A. Formoso,Andrés Ortiz*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 通过白噪声振幅调制刺激与EEG相位-幅度耦合分析，发现40 Hz节律的神经夹带缺陷可高精度预测发展性阅读障碍，并揭示右半球代偿性夹带机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展性阅读障碍影响全球5-12%人口，其生物学基础（尤其是口语语音任务缺陷）仍存争议。神经夹带（脑振荡与言语节律的耦合）对语音表征编码至关重要，因此作者试图探究非言语节律的脑夹带能否预测阅读障碍。

Method: 48名被试聆听按西班牙语语音单元速率调制的白噪声振幅。对EEG信号进行相位-幅度耦合（PAC）分析，采用Heights Ratio PAC指标预测阅读障碍；并在对照组中通过回归分析考察阅读成绩与夹带强度的关系。

Result: 40 Hz刺激下PAC指标可准确预测阅读障碍（最佳平衡准确率0.929，p=0.0069），提示阅读障碍者存在更强的音素夹带缺陷。对照组中阅读成绩与语言相关脑区夹带强度强相关；音素意识、阅读理解或快速自动化命名较差者，对40 Hz刺激表现出更强的右半球夹带。

Conclusion: 该方法可识别语言相关脑区附近的阅读障碍特异性夹带模式，以及右半球区域的代偿性夹带，为文献中假设的半球代偿机制提供了新证据。

Abstract: Developmental dyslexia (DLX) affects 5-12% of the global population and impairs learning to read. Its biological basis, particularly the difficulties observed in oral phonological tasks, remains debated. Entrainment, the coupling of brain oscillations to speech rhythms, is essential for encoding phonological representations. We investigated whether brain entrainment to non-speech rhythms could predict DLX in 48 participants listening to white noise amplitude-modulated at rates matching Spanish speech units. Phase-amplitude coupling (PAC) analysis of electroencephalography (EEG) signals yielded accurate DLX prediction for the 40 Hz stimulus using the Heights Ratio PAC measure (balanced accuracy 0.929, p = 0.0069, in the best case), indicating a stronger phonemic entrainment deficit in dyslexic individuals. Regression analysis in control (CTL) participants showed a strong dependence between reading performance scores and greater entrainment in language-associated brain regions. In particular, participants with poorer phonemic awareness, reading comprehension, or rapid automatized naming showed greater right-hemisphere entrainment to the 40 Hz stimulus. This finding adds evidence for the hemispheric compensatory mechanisms hypothesized in the literature. In conclusion, our methodology identifies dyslexia-specific entrainment patterns near language-associated brain regions, together with compensatory entrainment in right-hemisphere areas.

</details>
